Warm natural window daylight from the side, soft shadows, background gently overexposed, no glare on the board. Curious mood; calm, educational, teacher-like. Clean composition with generous black negative space, legible correctly spelled text, sharp focus, high detail, 16:9. Visual style: Chalkboard Clinic photorealistic chalkboard illustration. No people photographs, no color other than white and sky-blue chalk.
